namaḥ śrīkālacakrāya

iha khalu bahiradhyātmavidyābhyāsabhāvitacittasamtatir ācāryānu- pamarakșito nirālambakalpatattvopadeśabhāvanayā śrīkhasarpaņe dvādaśasamvatsarān anaisīt
tatra ca viśeṣādhigamam anāsādayato dīnamanaso nidrāsamupagatasya tasya vajrayoginyādeśo 'bhūt
gaccha putraka vikramapuram tatra tava viśeṣādhigamo bhaviṣyatīti sa ca tadādeśam śirasi nidhāya tatkālaśişyena sādhuputrena ma- hāpanditaśrīdhareņānugamyamāno vikramapuram agāt
tatra ca kasmimścit parvadivase ardharātrasamaye 'vadhūta samāsthāya bhagavatā sāksṣād upadiştah putraka idam tattvam iti tadupadeśalābhamātreņa samādhisamāpanno babhūva sa ca bha- gavāms tadaivāntarhitaḥ
atrāntare rātrir atītā sa ca samādher vyudasthāt
sa ca mahātmā śrīguruḥ sahakāriņam tatrabhavanta sa ca svākāśadhātāv uditaḥ suloko vibhāti lokeśvarapūrṇacandrah //
[ka] śrīśrīdharam svayam adhigatam upadistavān
sa ca śrīguruh para- makāruņikaḥ sarvathā bhājanam adhigamya svādhigatam mahāpan- ditam bhāskaradevam prasādīkṛtavān tato 'pi mahāpandita- śrīguruh kaṣāyakālasarvajño dharmākaraśāntim upadistavān
tato 'pi cānirvacanīyānavadyakarunādigunaratnākaraḥ śrīguroh l śrīgurucaranasaparyācatureņa mayāpi raviśriyā tattvam āsāditam iti gurupāramparyam

ataś ca svā- kāśadhātau śaradamalagaganatale udita āvirbhūtaḥ svālokah kalyāņasukhāloko lokeśvaro buddhaḥ padmapāņir vā l sa eva sarvajanāhlādakatvāt pūrṇacandrah

[LKC IV, 116ab] iti

yata evam tasmāt paramākṣarasukhasādhanābhiratena yoginā sad- gurūpadeśalabdhena dustasangaparivarjitena dhūmādinimittaparibhā- vitenādikarmikeņa madhyamāviśodhitena sakalasattvaikaputravatpara- masnehānubaddhacittena laukikalokottarasatyāśritena putrakalatrādi- svaśarīranirapeksakeņa mathavihāragurudravyopabhogabāhyabhūte- na buddhabodhisattvamārgāśritena maitrīkarunāmuditopekṣācatur- brahmavihāravihāriņā śāntikarmādilaukikasiddhisādhanābhilāsavarji- sadgurūpadeśalabdhena labdhaśrīgurusampradāyena

utpannakramayogas tattvam tattvasya sādhanam nānyat
di
etat tattvam prakatam abhisekapatale kāmānandas tu kampāksaram api ca catuskeņa yogah sa ekah pūrņā * śakty udbhavo vai bhavati ca paramānanda eva dvitīyah jvālā binduś ca ghūrmā punar api viramānanda evam trtīya oddā nādaś ca nidrā bhavati ca sahajānanda evam catu*rthah
iti
ato vikalpamārgena na tat tattvam
napi tattvasyātattvam nāma sādhanam bhavati
yady etat sādhanam na bhavati kim tad astv ity āha
utpattītyādi
tattvasya mahāsukha*sya sādhanam sādhyate 'neneti sādhanam tattvam eva nānyat padmādisamkalpanam
kim tat tattvam utpattikramamuktam garbhāvakramaņakramarahitam humphatkāra iti * mantrah
ādiśabdena mudrādikalpanā
etadrahitam tattvam
kim tad ity āha utpannakramayoga iti
utpanno nispanna ākāśabhāvanāgamyah l tasya kramo dhū- mā*dimārgah
tasya yogah prajñābimbena sahākṣarajñānodayasanga iti
idānīm āryādvayena sanidarśanabodhicittadarśanopāyam anupā- yapratiksepaņā*d aha
kasthastha ityādi
ya iva yadvat
agnir kāsthastho 'pi sadāgnir na dṛśyate chedabhedanopāyaiḥ
śarakāndapāņimathanāt tatrasthaḥ sa dṛśyate yadvat
tadvac cittābhāso na dṛśyate kalpabhāvanopāyaiḥ
tatraiva dṛśyate 'sau lalanārasanaikayogena ||
dhūmādinimittena prāṇāyāmena madhyavāhena
dṛśyate; nirāśrayas tāvan mā dṛśyatāṃ; kāsthastho 'pīty apiśabdārthah
chedabhedanopāyair iti
karane trtīyā
chedah * kuthārādibhiḥ
bhedana vṛkṣabhidādibhiḥ
upayo dvāraṃ
śarasya kāndo daṇḍah
paṇir hastah
tābhyāṃ mathanat so 'gnis tatrastho 'pīti tasmin nirmanthyakāsthe ti*sthatīti tatra- sthah
sa iva tadvat
cittasyābhāsaś cittābhās traidhātu- kadarśanaṃ na dṛśyate kalpabhāvanopāyair vikalpābhīksnā- bhimukhīkaranadvāraiḥ
asau cittābhā*so dṛśyate lalanā- rasanaikayogeneti
karane trtīyā
ekayogo 'dvayayogas tatraiva svacitta eveti
uktam paramārthasevāyām api
kāsthād dhavi*s toyam apīndukāntād dadhnā ghṛtam lohakulaṃ śilābhyaḥ
strīyonisangād avinastasaukhyaṃ gṛhṇantu yogyās tadupāyayogaiḥ ||
citte na kaścid bhavatāpanīyo na kse*paṇīyo na ca hānivṛddhī
citte svacittapratibhāsamātraṃ traidhātukaṃ toyagato yathārkah ||
iti
ekaṃ eva cittaṃ prajñābimbagataṃ anekaṃ iti bhāvaḥ
etad eva darśayann āha
yata evaṃ tasmād samā- muktakena yad uktam pratyāhārādipañcāngalābhināṃ dhyangasphutībhāvāya bodhicittasthirīkaraṇaikadakseneti tad vidyāvratena vajrāpātenaivordhvaśukrena ||
mārgeņānena sukham yogī prāpnoti sarvabuddhānām paramākṣarābhisādhanam sahajam vā sarvadūtīnām ||
evā*ha
dhūmetyādi
dhūma evādir yasya nimittasya tena l nimittam pratyayo yena sūcyate 'bhimatam tad iti
prānāyāmah kumbhaka ūrdhvādhas trinādīnirodhātmā
madhyavāhena cittanādyām vāho 'nupraveśaḥ prānavāyoh
vidyāvratena vidyāpradhānaṁ vratam brahmacaryam
vidyā svajinābhiṣiktā guṇavatī grāhyā
guruṇā abhidhānottare 'pi
samprāpya pukkasīm cānyām rūpayauvanamaṇḍitām
ekayogakṛtābhyāsām samayajñām drdhavratām ||
nirlajjām nihsprhām dhīrām vitrsnām nirahaṃkṛtām
vidyāvratam tayā sārdham ācaren mantravit svayam ||
iti
tato va*jrāpātena
vajrasyāpatanam stabdhyam teneti
ūrdhva- śūkreneti manipadmāt pratīpagatyā lalātam gatena
uktam ca
stabdalingaḥ sadā yogī ūrdhvaretāḥ sadā bhavet
mahāmudrāprasan*gena vajrāveśair adhisthitaḥ ||
ekavimśatsahasraiś ca șațśataiḥ paramākṣaraiḥ ||
iti ||
mārgeṇānena dhūmādiṣadangayogalakṣaṇena
yogy utpanna- kramayogo 'syāstīti sarva*buddhānām sukham nirindriyam paramākṣarābhisādhanam paramākṣaram abhisādhyate sa- mantān niṣpādyate yeneti sahajam tat prāpnoti
sarvadūtīnām niruddhānām dvāsaptatisahasra*nādīsvabhāvānām dākinīnām l śūnyam sahajam ity uktam
tribhavavarjitam anāhatam
na tu lokarūdhyā turyāvaraṇātmakam sahajam
śūnye hy aikyam ucyate sarvāvaraṇāpaga*mād iti vāśabdārthah ||
rūpādisaṃkalpanair maṇḍalacakrādibhāvanābhyāsaiḥ
sidhyati laukikasiddhiḥ kim punar iṣṭā mahāsiddhiḥ ||
mārgaḥ sadguruprasādato bhavati śuddhaśiṣyāṇām
nanv evam utpannakramayogas tattvam tattvasya sādhanaṃ nānyad ity uktam atas tad evāstu kim apareṇānupayoginety āha
rūpetyādi
lokas traidhātukam tatrabhavā laukikī cāsau siddhiś ceti siddhir akaniṣṭhabhuvanaparyantam aiśvaryaṃ sidhyati rūpādisaṃkalpanair iti rūpyate bādhyate iti rūpam
tad evādi yasya tasya kalpanaṃ mānasam karmeti
tatra rūpabhāvanā karmamudrā
ādiśabdena jñānaṃ tasya bhāvanā jñānamudrā
maṇḍalacakrādibhāvanābhyāsair iti maṇḍalam ādhāralakṣaṇaṃ cakraṃ devatāsamūha ādheyaḥ l ādi- śabdena mantramudrāsanādi l teṣāṃ bhāvanā 'bhimukhīkaraṇaṃ tasyābhyāsa ābhīkṣṇābhiniveśas tair iti
yady evam anenaiva mārgeṇa sarvasiddhiḥ sidhyati
alam dhūmādimārgopadeśenety āha
kim iti mahatī cāsau siddhiś ceti mahāsiddhir mahāmudrāsiddhiḥ l iṣṭā lokottarā
kim punaḥ kim u l siddhir iti dhūmādimargopadeśaṃ antareṇa naivety arthaḥ ||
nanv ayaṃ mārgopadeśaḥ kim sarveṣām eva samyaksaṃbo- dhiprāptisamudyatamatīnāṃ sarvasmād eva bhavati
āhosvit keṣāñcid eva śuddhānāṃ jagadarthakaraṇakuśalotsāhināṃ kutaścid evety āha
mārga ityādi
sadguruḥ śrīgurus tasya lakṣaṇaṃ paramārthasevayāṃ vistareṇākhyātaṃ yathā
dhyāyī bhavadhyānavimuktasaṅgaḥ kāmī mahākāmanibaddhacittaḥ
mahāvratī dvīndriyasaukhyayoge mudrāprasaṅge 'py avinastarāgaḥ ||
candrārkamārgapravīṇastayogaḥ prajñānale yogabalāt praviṣṭaḥ
anantasukhyāmṛtaseka eva sa śrīgurur vajradharo 'ghahantā ||
iti vistaraḥ
tasya prasādas tattvopadeśānugrahaḥ
tasmād bhava- yesāṁ sattveṣu kṛpā parakāryaśubhodyamo nityam ||
anyasmai dattam idaṁ sumārgaratnaṁ pramattasattvāya
hriyate kuviṣayacauraiḥ svagṛhāranyaṁ praviṣṭasya ||
vicikitsākaukṛtyanidrā-ālasyauddhatyacauraiś ca
hriyate sumārgaratnaṁ kuṭumbagahanaṁ praviṣṭasya ||
ty utpadyate l keṣāṁ śuddhaśiṣyāṇāṁ l śuddhāḥ sekādibhiḥ prahīṇaprākṛtāhaṁkāramalāś ca te śiṣyāḥ parārthakaraṇādibhir anuśāsanīyāś ceti
uktaṁ mahāmudrātilake
viṣaṁ mantrādibhir yadvat śodhyamānaṁ rasāyanam
tadvat sekādibhiḥ śiṣyam aśuddhaṁ śuddhimad bhaved ||
iti
teṣāṁ parakāryaśubhodyamaḥ kalyāṇotsāha iti ||
idānīṁ labdhamārgāṇāṁ pramādināṁ mārgāpaharaṇanimittam āha
anyetyādi
idaṁ anantaroktaṁ buddhatvaṁ sukhena mṛgyate 'nviṣyate yeneti mārgam
ratiṁ tanotīti ratnaṁ cintāmaṇiratnam iva ratnaṁ durlabhatvāc cintitārthaphalapradatvāc ca sumar- garatnaṁ l paścād viśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ
prramattaḥ pramādacārī hitasukhānapekṣaḥ
sadgatiṣu sīdatīti sattvaḥ
anyo yaḥ
kṛpādiguṇavikalaḥ parahitasukhānudyatamatiḥ
tasya praviṣṭasya svasya gṛham dārās tad evāraṇyaṁ krūrasattvāśrayam
kuviṣayacaurair iti viṣayantīndriyāṇy eṣv iti viṣayā rūpādayo bāhyabhogās te ca kutsitā alpāsvādatvād vipakṣatvān mūr- chācittenādharmasevanāc ca l ta eva caurās tair dattaṁ api sumārgaratnaṁ hriyate cauryata iti ||
na kevalam ebhiḥ svagṛhāranyaṁ praviṣṭasya sumārgaratnam hriyate
kuṭumbagahanaṁ praviṣṭasya vicikitsādicauraiś ca
kuṭumbaḥ kalatraputrapautrādir gahanaṁ vanaṁ l tribhir prāṇātipātamithyā-adattaparadārarūkṣapaiśunyam
saṃbhinnavaco 'bhidhyāvyāpādakudṛṣṭicauraiś ca ||
satpāpakopapāpakahatyādrohendriyābhisamsaktiḥ
pañcakapañcabhir ebhiś caurai ratnam sadā hriyate ||
api pratyayair niścite 'pi sumārgaratne saṃśayo vicikitsā kim ayam sumārgo na veti
kaukṛtyam kutsitaṃ karma mayā kṛtam ato nāhaṃ mārgaṃ sākṣātkariṣya ity evamākāram
nidrā svāpaḥ kāyamanasor akarmanyatāpādanalakṣaṇaṃ styānamiddhaṃ iti yāvat
ālasyam anutsāha auddhatyam vikṣiptacittatā nagarādicitta- bhramaṇaṃ iti yāvat
tad amī samādhimālā anayāryayākhyātā mārgaṃ bhāvayatām iti ||
na kevalam ebhiḥ samānair hriyate prāṇātipātādibhir api
rat- naṃ iti sumārgaratnaṃ
sadā kālatraye
prāṇātipātaḥ sattvavadhaḥ
mithyety anṛtavacaḥ
adatteti paradravyāharaṇaṃ
paradāreti kāmamithyācāraḥ
rūkṣavacaḥ paramarmodghātanaṃ
paiśunya- vacaḥ kliṣṭacittasya parabhedavacanaṃ
saṃbhinnavacaḥ kā- mādyupasaṃhitasya hāhaihehītyādirāgajanakaṃ vacaḥ
abhidhyā paradravye visamaspṛhā
vyāpādo vadhacittaṃ
kudṛṣṭir nāstidṛṣṭiḥ ||
tathā
satpāpakaṃ hiṃsādipañcakaṃ
upapāpakapañcakaṃ dyūtādipañcakaṃ
gohatyādipañcakaṃ mitradrohādipañcakaṃ
cakṣurādīndriyābhisamsaktipañcakaṃ iti
pañca parimaṇaṃ asyeti pañcakānāṃ pañca tair eva caurair iti
tathā
hiṃsāsatyaṃ parastrīṃ tyajasva paradhanaṃ madyapānaṃ tathaiva saṃsāre vajrapāśaḥ svakuśalanidhanaṃ pāpam etāni pañca
dyūtaṃ sāvadhyabhojyaṃ kuvacanapathanaṃ bhūtadaityendra- dharmam gobālastrīnarāṇāṃ tridaśanaraguroḥ pañcahatyāṃ na kuryāt atyantakhānapānair nānābhogair anekacaurais taiḥ
vīryavato 'pi hriyate pramādamūrchāṁ gatasyaiva ||
svayam eva na hi parīkṣaka itarasya vitasya darśayej jñātum
bhavati mahārghaṁ na bhavati tadvākyān muñcate ratnam ||
drohaṁ mitraprabhūṇāṁ tridaśanaraguroḥ saṁghaviśvāsināṁ ca āsaktis tv indriyāṇām iti bhuvanapateḥ pañcavimśad vratāni ||
iti
pāpakaṁ karma khyātam antarāyakaram atra gṛhītam pañcānantaryakāriṇām apīti ||
na kevalaṁ ebhiḥ sumararatnaṁ hriyate atyanta- khānapānair nānā bhogais ceti khānaṁ dibhakṣaṇam
pānaṁ surādipānaṁ atyantaṁ dharmeneti
bhuñjyanta iti bhogāḥ srakcandanādayaḥ
nānā vicitraḥ
vīryavato 'pi mārgabhāvanotsāhino na kevalam anantaroktasyādṛdhavīryasya vīryavato 'pīty apiśabdārthaḥ
pramādo 'tya*ntakhānapānādyakalyāṇamitrāsangaḥ tasmān mūrchaṁ cetaso vikalatāṁ gatasya hriyata eveti
bhava- bhogādiratir uktā 'ntarāyakarī kuśalakāriṇām apīti ||
nanv evaṁ mārgaratnasya parīkṣakam abhisandhāyoktā antarāyakarā doṣāḥ
tasya ca mārgasya svayam aparīkṣakasya tu kā värttety āha
svayam ityadi
hir yasmāt svayam aparī*ksakas tasmād itarasyānyasya vitasya mahāpāpakṛtasya darśayed bhavati mahārghaṁ mahāratnam idaṁ iti jñātum
sa āha na bhavati atas tadvākyād vitavacanān muñcata e*veti
evaṁ eva yebhyaḥ kārayati mahāratnaparīkṣāṃ suratnavettrbhyah
teṣāṃ viśuddhavākyaiḥ svakīyaratnaṃ hi vijñeyam || [15]
māraḥ karoti vighnaṃ rūpaiḥ saṃbuddhabodhisattvānāṃ
pitṛmātṛduhitṛbhaginīputrabhrātṛstabhāryāṇāṃ || [16]
tasmāt sadgurudattaṃ sumārgaratnaṃ hi yatnataḥ śiṣyaiḥ
kartavyaṃ atisuguptaṃ caurakutumbaṃ parityajya || [17]
duḥprajñajātīyaḥ pāpamitravacanād anarghaṃ sumārgaratnaṃ prā- ptam api muñcatīti pāpamitropasaṃgamanadoṣa ukto mārgaparī- kṣārthinām apīti ||
na*nv evaṃ kathaṃ mārgaratnaṃ vijñeyaṃ svayam aparīkṣakeṇeti cet
satyaṃ l sa cet kārayati mahāratnaparīkṣāṃ yebhyaḥ suratnavettrbhyaḥ sakāśāt teṣāṃ viśuddhavā*kyair avisaṃ- vādakavacanair hi sphuṭaṃ vijñeyaṃ niścetavyam anargham idam iti
evaṃ eva kulaputreṇa prāpto mārgaḥ kalyāṇamitravacanād anarghaṃ idam iti parī*kṣya l anubaddhavyo na moktavya iti kalyāṇamitrāśrayaḥ śreyān mārgabhāvakānāṃ iti ||
tad evaṃ mārgabhāvanāyāṃ prasthitasyāpi na cittaṃ sthirībhavati māra*karmatyāgaṃ vinety atas tad eva darśayann āha
māra ityādi māro hi pāpīyān buddhādirūpaṃ nirmāya bhāvakasyāntarāyaṃ karoti yathā nāyaṃ mārgo bodhaye nānena kaści*n niryātītyādi veditavyaṃ mārakarmaparivarte ||
evaṃ mārakarmadoṣaṃ upakṣipya sarvam upasaṃharann āha
tasmād ityādi l hir yasmād etan mārgāpahāranimittaṃ asti tasmāc caurakutumba*ṃ upalakṣaṇaṃ vicikitsādikam api tya- ktvā yatnataḥ sadgurudattaṃ mārgaratnaṃ guptaṃ kāryam vīrakramo na mārgaḥ svādhisthānakramaś ca mokṣāya
suviśuddhakrama eko mokṣāya samdarśito buddhaih || [18]
saukhyena samgṛhītāḥ pañcānantaryakāriņo yena I sekārthena jinendraiḥ prajñopāyātmake tantre || [19]
vīrakramo na bāhye dehe prāņaksayo hy asāv uktaḥ
svādhisthānam śūnye traidhātukadarśanam nāma || [20]
śiṣyair iti mārgarakşoktā ll nanv aparo 'pi margo 'sti mokṣāya na vety a*ha
vire- tyādi vīrakramo bāhyaraudradevatāpūjālambanādiḥ svādhi- sthāņakramaś cittena śāntadevatāyogasukhālambano mokṣāya na deśito buddhaih
kah punas tarhi deśita ity a*ha
suviśud- dhakrama eka iti utpannakramayogo 'dvitīyah ||
katham etaj jñāyate iti ced āha
saukhyenetyādi
yena kāraṇena jinendraih samgṛhītā viśve samu*panītāḥ pañcānan- taryakāriņa iti
anantaryāni mātṛpitrarhadvadhāḥ samghabhedo
dustacittena tathāgate rudhirotpādaś ceti
etadanyatarenāvī*cau kalpam anantaram evāsyata ity anantaryam ucyate
saukhyena sukhopāyena sekārthena caturthena
anyathā pañcānantaryakāriņām vīrakrameņa raudra*karmaviratih svādhisthānena vardhitasamsārasu- khavāsanāsamtatena mokṣāvāptih
tena kāraneneti
nanu yadi idam kramadvayam vīrasvādhisthānākhyam akra- mam e*va manyate tadā bhagavatā kimartham uktam iti ced āha l vīretyādi hir yasmād dehe prāņaksayo hy asāv uktas tasmād vīrakramo na bāhye
nanv evam bāhye * svādhi- sthānam spastam astv iti cen na l yasmāt svasya cittasyādhi- sthānam pratibhāsaḥ
tac ca śūnye traidhātukadarśanam nā- samsāre nihsāre buddhatvam phalam tatkṣayāt tatah pumsāṁ
kadalīphalam iva pakvam kadalīnāśena sambhavati || [21]
vṛścikakulīrasattvā yasyah syus te vināśinas tasyāḥ
evam bhavati vināśi tad yogacittam māyāyāḥ || [22]
advayam acalam ananvayam istam paramākşaram mahārāgam
meti prasiddham suviśuddhakramavyavasthoktā jagadartha- kā*riņām apīti
nanu svādhisthānam traidhātukasya darśanam uktam taddarśanāt tasyānivṛtteh samsāranivṛttilaksanam buddhatvam katham iva sambhavatīty āha
samsāra ityādi
sam*sare traidhātuke nihsare mṛsāmosadharmake buddhatvam sarvajñatādiguna- vibhūtilaksanam phalam sāram bhavati tatkṣayāt samsārakṣayād anantaram
katham ivety āha kada*līphalam iveti l yathā nihsārāyām kadalyām sāram phalam pakvam bhavati kadalīnāśena evam buddhatvam apīti
nanu buddhatvasya bodhicittam sādhanam isyate
ta*ttvam tattvasya sādhanam iti vacanāt l tac ca māyotpannatvān māyāyā avidyātvena vartanād atattvam katham buddhatvasādhanam bhavatīty āha
vṛściketyādi
vṛści*kāḥ śūkakītāḥ kulīrāḥ karkatā yasyā mātur bhaveyus tasyā vināśinas te yathā evam yogacittam yogaḥ prajñopāyasamāpattis tasmin yac cittam caturtho 'bhisekah sa*myrtyotpannam yasyā māyāyā avidyācyutidharminyāḥ sakāśāt tasyā vināśi tad bhavatīti māyopaśamena samsārasyāpunarāvṛttir uktā tattvayogināṁ āśvāsanārtha*m iti
nanu bodhicittam śukradhātur ihocyate
tat katham ajadasya buddhatvasya paramākṣarajñānasya sādhanam uktam
satyam uktam
śukrākṣarasvabhāvena sādhayet paramākşaram | iti * vacanāt
ata evāsya tattvasya yogacittasya paramākṣararūpeņa sodaśacatuhprabhedam prakatayann āha
advayam ityādi
dvayam bhāvābhāvābhāvam jñānaṁ sattvaṁ samantabhadraṁ ca || [23]
araṇo mahāraṇaś ca sahajaḥ śrībodhicittabindudhāraḥ
śrīkālacakravajraḥ prajñopāyātmako yogaḥ || [24]
evaṁ yo vajrayogo 'parimitasugataiḥ sevito deśitaś ca saṁgīto bodhisattvair varakuliśadharair mantravidbhiḥ samastaiḥ || [25]
ādau saṁsevanīyo gurur api samayī vajrayānādhirūḍhas tattvadhyāyī tv alubdho vyapagatakaluṣaḥ kṣāntiśīlo 'dhvavartī
śiṣyāṇāṁ mārgadātā narakabhayaharas tattvato brahmacārī mārāṇāṁ vajradaṇḍaḥ sa ca dharaṇitale vajrasattvaḥ prasiddhaḥ || [LKĊ III, 2]
padmād na calatīty acalam
na vidyate 'nvayaḥ prajñopāyāṁśo 'syety ananvayam
anuttarasukhapradatvād iṣṭam iti caturtho yogaḥ

de kho na yi sgrub thabs gzhan yod min
[] ji ltar me ni shing la rtags gnas kyang
bcad dang dbye ba'i thabs kyis mi mthong ste
|| gtsub shing gtsub stan lag pas bsrub pa las
de la gnas pa yang ni mthong 'gyur ba
[] de bzhin sems kyi snang bar rtog pa yi
bsgom pa'i thabs rnams kyis ni mi mthong ste
|| de nyid du 'di la la nā dang ni
ro ma gcig tu sbyor bas mthong par 'gyur
[] du ba la sogs mtshan ma rnams kyang ni
srog rtsol gyis ni dbu mar 'bab' pa dang
| rig ma'i brtul zhugs kyis ni rdo rje ni
Itung min de bzhin steng du khu ba ste
[] | lam 'di rnams kyis sangs rgyas rnams kyi ni
bde ba mchog tu 'gyur ba med ces pa
|| mngon par sgrub pa 'am pho nya mo kun gyis
lhan cig skyes pa rnal 'byor pas thob 'gyur
[] | gzugs la sogs par rtog pa dkyil 'khor gyi
'khor lo sgom pa goms par gyur pas ni
|| 'jig rten pa yi dngos grub 'grub 'gyur gyi
'dod pa'i dngos grub chen po ga la zhig
[] gang rnams sems can rnams la brtse ba yis
gzhan kyi bya ba dge ba rtag brtson pa'i
|| slob ma dag pa rnams la lam rnams ni
bla ma dam pa'i drin las 'byung ba yin
[] rnams kyis kyang
|| gnyen 'dun 'thibs po'i nang du zhugs pa yi
|| lam bzangs rin po che ni 'phrog par byed
| srog gcod ma byin gzhan gyi bud med dang
brdzun dang tshig rtsub dang ni phra ma dang
|| 'khyal bar gyur pa'i tshig dang brnab sems dang
gnod sems lta ba ngan pa'i chom rkun dang
| yod pa'i sdig dang nye ba'i sdig pa dang
gsod 'khu dbang po kun nas chags pa ste
|| chom rkun Inga pa Inga po 'di rnams kyis
rin po che ni rtag tu 'phrog par byed
| shin tu bza' dang btung dang sna tshogs pa'i
longs spyod du ma'i chom rkun de rnams kyis
| brtson 'grus ldan du zin kyang bag med cing
brgyal bar gyur pa nyid la 'phrag par byed |
| gang phyir rang nyid rtog pa po min pa |
rin thang chen por 'gyur ram shes ched du ||
g.yon can gzhan la ston pa min zhes pa'i |
de'i tshig gis rin chen 'dor bar byed |
gang nams na chen bzang po rig pa rnams |
rin cen chen po yongs su rtog tu gzhug ||
de rnams kyi ni rnam ngag dag rnams kyis |
rang gi rin chen rnam par shes par bya |
I rdzogs sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang |
pha dang ma dang bu mo sring mo dang |
| bu dang spun zla 'dod pa'i chung ma yi |
gzugs rnams kyis ni bdud rnams gegs byed do |
I de phyir bla ma dam pas gnang ba yi |
lam bzang rin chen slob ma rnams kyis ni ||
chom rkun rtsa lag yong su btang byas te |
'dang pas shin tu legs par sba bar bya |
I dpa' bo'i rim pa bdag byin brlab pa yi |
rim pa 'ang thar pa'i ched du lam ma yin ||
shes tu rnam dag rim pa gcig pu ni |
thar pa'i ched du sangs rgyas rnams kyis bstan |
| mtshams med Inga po byed pa rnams kyang ni |
dbang bskur don ni bde ba gang zhig gis ||
thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid rgyud du ni |
rgyal ba'i dbang pos yang dag gzung bar 'gyur |
I dpa' bo'i rim ni phyi rol la min te |
lus la srog zad nyid la 'di gsungs so ||
bdag byin brlab pa'i rim po stong pa la |
khams gsum po ni mthong bar gyur pa'i mig |
| 'khor ba snying po med de zad gyur pa |
de las skyes bu'i sangs rgyas nyid 'brus bu ||
chu shing gi ni 'bras bu smin pa dag |
chu shing nyams las yang dag 'byung ba bzhin |
| sdig srin dang ni shing srin sems can rnams |
gang la byung ba de rnams des nyams byed ||
de bzhin rnal 'byor rnams kyi sems des ni |
sgyu ma rnam par nyams par byed par 'gyur |
| gnyis med g.yo ba med dang gnyis rtogs med |
'dod dang mchog tu mi 'gyur 'dod chags che ||
dngos dang dngos po med pa dang |
ye shes dang ni sems pa kun tu bzang |
| sgra 'thab med dang sgra che ba dang ni |
lhan skyes dpal ldan byang sems thig le 'dzin ||
dpal ldan dus kyi 'khor lo rdo rje dang |
shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid rnal 'byor ro |
I de ltar rdo rje'i rnal 'byor gang zhig dpag med bde gshegs rnams kyis bsten cing brtan pa dang ||
byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje mchog 'dzin gsang sngags rig pa kun gyis yang dag bsdus gyur pa'o|
dang por yang dag brten par bya ba bla ma 'ang dam tshig Idan zhing rdo rje'i theg la lhag par gnas ||
de kho na nyid sgom zhing ma chags dri ma rnams bral bzod pa'i ngang tshul lam la 'jug ||
slob ma rnams la lam ni sbyin byed dmyal ba'i 'jigs 'phrog de nyid las ni tshangs spyod ldan ||
bdud rnams la ni rdo rje'i dbugs pa de yang 'dzin ma'i gzhi la rdo rje sems dpar rab grags pa'o |
| lha'i dmigs pa gang yin mngon sum dang ni rjes su dpag pa rnam gnyis 'dir 'gyur te ||
mngon sum de kho na nyid sbyor las mkha' la skar ma bzhin du du ma longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku ||
mngon sum min na rjes su dpag pa shi ba'i lus bzhin de kho na nyid min pas rtags pa gang ||
de ni ri mo sogs la blta bya yongs su ma smin blo can rnal 'byor rnams kyi sgom pa'i don |
'dir sems can rnams kyi bsams pa'i dbang gis rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi lha'i dmigs pa gang yin mngon sum dang ni rjes su dpag pa rnams kyis gnyis su 'gyur ba ste |
mngon sum dang rjes su dpag pa de dag las mngon sum gang yin pa de ni de kho na nyid kyi rnal 'byor gyis mkha' la skar ma bzhin du 'gyur te |
skar ma'i tshogs bzhin du du ma longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku zhes pa ni sha la sogs pa'i mig gis gzung bar bya ba srid pa gsum dang dus gsum rmi lam dang sgyu ma dang mtshungs pa'o ||
'dir dang por las dang po pa'i mal 'byor pas mngon par shes pa med par sha'i mig gis gzugs brnyan mthong ngo ||
de nas mngon par shes pa'i mtshams kyi dbang gis lha'i mig gis mthong go ||
de nas 'dod chags dang bral ba'i mtshams kyi dbang gis sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis gzigs so ||
de nas byang chub sems dpa'i mtshams kyi dbang gis shes rab kyi spyan gyis gzigs so ||
de nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi mtshams kyi dbang gis ye shes kyi spyan gyis gzigs te |
lhag ma kun las rnam grol zhing ||
zhes so ||
de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i sha la sogs pa'i spyan Inga po rnams ni stong pa nyid gzigs pa'i ched du gsungs te ||
sems can gzhan rnams ni stong pa nyid Ita ba'i yul dmus long ngo zhes pa de kho na nyid sgom pa'i nges pa'o ||
de kho na nyid ma yin pa'i sgrub par byed pa la mngon sum min na rjes su dpag pa shi ba'i lus bzhin de kho na nyid min pas brtags pa gang ||
de ni ri mo sogs la blta bya ||
zhes pa ni sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ri mo dang sku gzugs byas pa dang lugs ma 'am dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo bris nas nges pa'i rnam par blta bar bya ste |
byis pa'i rnal 'byor pa dman pa rnams kyi sgom pa'i don du'o zhes pa rnam par rtog pa sgom pa'i nges pa'o ||
bdud rtsi Inga la sogs dang chu gter rdo rje rnams dang sngags kyi bzlas pa sogs kyis bsnyen pa ste ||
so sor sdud pa sogs dang rdo rje padma las skyes bdud rtsi yis ni nye bar sgrub par 'gyur ||
dga' ba la sogs rnams kyis rdo rje gsum dang chu skyes ro mnyam gyur pa bsgom pa sgrub par 'gyur ||
shes rab 'grogs la 'pho med 'gyur ba phra mo'i rnal 'byor dag las nges par sgrub pa chen po'o |
'dir las dang po pas dang por sgrub thabs kyi cho gas bsnyen pa byas te |
phyi rol du bdud rtsi Inga ni bshang ba la sogs pa'o ||
sogs pa'i sgras go ku la sogs pa ste |
lha mnyes pa'i don du de rnams zos pas bsnyen pa'o ||
nang du bdud rtsi Inga ni phung po Inga rnams so ||
sogs pa'i sgras ni dbang po Inga rnams sgron ma Inga'o ||
de rnams la ltos pa med pa ni bsnyen pa ste l |
lus dang rdzas la sred pa yongs su spangs pa'o ||
bsnyen pa des lha rnams mchog ster bar 'gyur gyi bshang ba la sogs pa zos pas ni ma yin no ||
chu gter rdo rje rnams kyis zhes pa lus kyi longs spyod la ltos pa med pa dang ||
ngag gi longs spyod la ltos pa med pa dang |
sems kyi longs spyod la ltos pa med pa dang |
'phro ba'i longs spyod kyi bde ba la ltos pa med pa ste lus dang ngag dang sems dang tshangs par spyod pa'i sdom pa'o zhes pa'i don to ||
'dis lha mchog sbyin par 'gyur gyi srid pa'i longs spyod la chags pas ni ma yin no zhes so ||
sngags kyi bzlas pa sogs kyis zhes pa la ||
'dir sngags kyi bzlas pa zhes bya ba ni srog sdom pa ste |
sogs pa'i sgras ni dbyung ba dang dgang ba dang bum pa can gyi rnal 'byor ni rtag tu bsnyen pa'o ||
des lha mchog ster bar 'gyur gyi srog ma bsdams pas ni ma yin no zhes pa'i nges pa'i don to ||
drang ba'i don gyis bgrang phreng la sogs pas bzlas pa la sogs pa bya ste ||
thun mong gi dngos grub kyi don du'o l I da ni nye ba'i bsgrub pa brjod par bya ste ||
so sor zhes pa la sogs pa'o ||
so sor sdud pa zhes pa la 'dir 'khor ba pas rnams kyi sdud pa mig
la sogs pa'i dbang po rnams kyis gzugs la sogs pa'i yul 'dzin pa'o
l de yongs su spong ba ni so sor sdud par brjod par bya ste | stong pa nyid kyi gzugs la mig la sogs pa'i gzhan rnams kyis yul 'dzin pa ni nye ba'i bsgrub pa'o
|| de bzhin du bsam gtan dang srog rtsol dang 'dzin pa ste | rdo rje padma las skyes bdud rtsi yis ni nye bar bsgrub pa ste nges pa'i don gyis so
|| drang ba'i don gyis ni phyi rol du lha spros pas so zhes pa nye ba'i sgrub pa'o
I da ni sgrub pa gsungs pa | dga' ba zhes pa la sogs pa ste | 'dir dga' ba la sogs pa rnams kyis rdo rje gsum po sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi thig le rnams te | de rnams chu skyes su ro mnyam par gyur pa sgom pa bsgrub par 'gyur te snying ga dang lte ba dang gsang bar thig le rnams gnas pa'o zhes bya ba'i don to
|| de Itar bsgrub pa po'o'
I de nas bsgrub pas chen po ni shes rab sbyar la gang gi tshe 'pho ba med pa'i bde bar 'gyur ba de'i tshe sgrub pa chen po ste | phra mo'i rnal 'byor las ni zhes pa nges pa'i don gyis su shum na'i rtsa'i steng du khu ba yang dag par sbyor ba las nges pa'i don gyi bsgrub pa chen por brjod par bya'o
I drang ba'i don gyis ni shes rab kyi chos 'byung gi sna'i rtse mor yungs kar la sogs pa'o zhes pa nges pa'o
|| de ltar sgrub pa chen por 'gyur ro
|| dang por stong nyid byang chub nges par slar yang de nas zla ba thig le yang dag 'dzin
de nas gzugs nams skyed cing rab mchog ro'i rigs rnams kyi ni ge god pa nyid
'di ni spyi yi bsnyen pa chu yi'i gter gyi rdo rje rnams kyis sgrub pa 'bring po yang
'dir thabs rnam bzhir 'gyur te dman dang brtan pa sgrub pa'i yan lag la ni de bzhin nyid
K'dir bskyed pa'i rim pas dang por stong nyid byang chub ces bya ba ni srog chags nams shi ba'i mthar phung po yongs su dor zhing skye ba'i cha shas can gyi phung po 'dzin pa las par gang yin pa stong pa nyid kyi skad cig gcig po srid pa gsum mthong ba ni stong zhes brjod bya'o
|| nges par ni gdon mi za bar ro
|| skad cig de'i phyi nas zla ba thig le yang dag 'dzin
zhes 'dir kun gzhi rnam par shes pas ma'i mngal du khu ba'i thig le rnams 'dzin pa ni yang dag 'dzin no
|| de 'og tu khu ba la sogs pa bzung ba de nas gzugs rnams bskyed pa zhes bya ba ni zla ba bdun pa rnams kyis mngal rdzogs pa ste | lus rdzogs pa'o zhes pa'i don to
|| de nas rab mchog ro'i rigs rnams kyis
|| zhes pa phung po drug po rnams kyis mig la sogs pa rnams kyi yi ge dgod pa ste | gzugs la sogs pa'i yul la rab tu 'jug pa'o zhes pa'o
|| de bzhin du lha'i rnal 'byor la yang brtags pa'i bdag nyid bsgom par bya ste las dang po pas so
|| 'di ni spyi'i bsnyen pa'o
|| chu'i gter gyi rdo rje nams kyis zhes pa lus dang ngag dang sems dang ye shes kyi rdo rje rdzogs pa rnams kyis bsgrub pa 'bring po yang ste srog rdzogs pa'o
|| 'dir thabs rnam pa bzhir 'gyur te zhes pa bsnyen pa'i yan lag dang nye ba'i bsgrub pa'i yan lag la dman pa dang brtan pa'o
|| sgrub pa'i yan lag la de bzhin kho nar lo bcu drug gi mtshams su'o zhes pa nges pa'o l
| 'dir dman pa ni skyes pa'i byis pa'o
|| so skyes pas 'bring po'i byis pa'o l
I so lhung nas gzhon nu'o
|| slar yang so skyes nas | lo bcu drug gi rjes la dar ma ste bu skyed par byed pa nyid kyi phyir ro zhes pa'o
|| de bzhin du lha thams cad yan lag rnam pa bzhir rnal 'byor pas bsgom par bya'o zhes pa ni 'jig rten pa'i bden pa'i nges pa'o l
da ni don dam pa'i bden pas sangs rgyas kyi gzugs rdzogs par brjod par bya ste
|| 'dir dang po stong nyid byang chub ces pa ni mun pa la cung zad kyang bsam par mi bya'o
|| de nas | zla ba thig le yang dag 'dzin
zhes pa ni du ba la sogs pa'i mtshan ma thig le'i mthar thug pa 'dzin pa'o
|| de nas gzugs rnams bskyed cing zhes pa dang | thig le de nas sna tshogs gzugs mthong ba ni gzugs skye ba'o
|| rab mchog ro'i rigs rnams kyis
|| zhes pa sgrib pa med pa'i phung po drug rnams kyis so
|| yi ge dgod pa zhes pa ni nyi tshe ba'i phung po dang khams dang skye mched la sogs pa rnams 'gog pa'o
|| de'i phyir sa thob pa ni dman pa la sogs pa'i dbye bas 'gyur te | ji srid sa bcu gnyis kyi dbang phyug tu ma gyur gyi bar du'o
|| de'i phyir | bden don rnam pa bcu gnyis ldan
I de nyid rnam pa bcu drug rig
I rnam pa nyi shus byang chub pa
| rnam par sangs rgyas kun rig mchog
| ces so
|| de bzhin du | stong par du ba la sogs lam bla ma'i nges pa'i dbang gis gzugs brnyan mtshams su bsgom par bya
de nas rtsa ba'i 'khor lo rnams su srog dang thur sel rlung ni brtan pa 'ang byed pa ste
|| phyi nas zla.ba'i 'gog pa bza' bzhe ni byed de byang chub sems ni nal 'byor pa yis so
|| de nas gang zhig cung zad 'dod pa nyin zhag 'ga' yis skye ba 'di la thob par 'gyur
| zhes so
| lam gyi rgyal po 'di sgom pa'i rim pa'i man ngag mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas su gsungs pa | 'di lta ste | du ba la sogs sgoms nas ni
I sems ni g.yo ba med byas te
| dbu ma sbyangs pa byas nas ni
| mchog tu mi 'gyur bsgrub par bya
I padmar rdo rje rab bkod nas
| srog gi thig ler gzhug par bya
| 'khor lor thig le'o thig le yi
I g.yo ba rdo rjer dgag par bya
| rtag rtu rtag rengs rnal 'byor pa l
rtag tu steng du khu bar 'gyur
| phyag rgya che dang 'grogs pa yi l
rdo rje 'bebs pas byin gyis brlab
drug brgya rdzogs pas rgyal po che l rdo rje sems dpar rang nyid 'gyur
rnam pa gzhan du na l shes rab ma dang 'grogs mi bya
ji srid bcu ni ma shar par | rnal 'byor pa ni phyi'i g.yeng ba | yongs su chad na mtshan ma rnams | so sor sdud pa rgyal ba'i dbang por 'gyur te rnam pa bcu'o bsam gtan mi skyod pa nyid do
|| srog rtsol yang ni ral gri can no slar yang nam pa bcu'o 'dzin pa phyag na rin chen nyid
|| g.yung pa mo la ni rjes su dran pa padma 'dzin 'gyur dpal ldan ting 'dzin 'khor lo can
|| re re dbye bar Inga rnams kyis te slar yang gang las a sogs ka sogs nams kyis phye ba'o | so sor sdud pa lus la yul dang yul can bcu nams rab tu 'jug pa med pa'oll bsam gtan yang ni shes rab rtog dang dpyod dga' dang g.yo med bde ba sems rtse gcig pa'o
|| srog gi rtsol ba yang ni lam ni gnyis po nyams zhing dbu mar srog ni zhugs par 'gyur
|| thig ler srog rab zhugs shing gnyis ka'i bgrod pa bcom pa sems rtse gcig pa 'dzin pa'o | gtum mo'i snang ba gang zhig nges par lus dang nam mkha' la' 'gyur ba
rjes su dran par 'gyur ||
shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid kyis ni 'gyur med bde ba'i dbang gis ye shes gzugs la ting nge 'dzin ||
'di rnams dman pa la sogs dbye ba nams kyis sna tshogs gtso bo'i sgrub thabs rnam gsum 'gyur ||
phyag rgya gsum dang gnas skabs gsum ni rnam gsum bgrod pa'i dbang gis las dang kun rtog mchog |
so sor sdud pas rnal 'byor pa ni yul dang rnam bral stegs rnams kun gyis byin gyis brlab ||
mngon shes Inga ni thob par 'gyur te mi yi bdag po btag sbyor bas dag pa'o ||
srog gis rtsol bas dag pa ri bong can dang nyi ma dang bral byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis mchod ||
bdud dang nyon mongs la sogs nyams shing stobs bcu la ni 'jug 'gyur 'dzin pa'i stobs kyis so |
rjes su dran pa yang dag dri med 'od kyi dkyil 'khor ye shes gzugs las so ||
de nas ting nge 'dzin gyi dag pa nyin zhag 'ga' zhig rnams kyis ye shes lus 'grub po ||
so sor sdud pa sogs kyis gal te sngags pa sogs kyi 'dod pa grub par ma gyur na ||
nā da goms pas chu skyes su gnas rdo rje nor bu stobs kyis thig le bkag nas bsgrub pa |
bsnyen pa la ni dang po'i rnal 'byor rnam bcu mkha' la 'khor lo can gyi khro bo'i lta bas so ||
bgegs mthar byed pa'i blta bas bdud rtsi'i lam du son pa yis kyang nye ba'i bsgrub la yan lag drug ||
shes pa la spros zla ba thig le rdo rje nor bur gnas pa'i 'gyur med gsum ni sgrub pa la ||
bde ba mi nyams gcig pu zhi ba lhan skyes ye shes rnal 'byor 'dir ni sgrub pa chen po la'o |
da ni rnal 'byor yan lag drug brjod par bya ste so sor sdud pa zhes so ||
las dang po la rgyal po'i dbang po zhes pa ye shes kyi phung po'o||
de yang mtshan ma'i dbye ba rnam pa bcu ste |
du ba dang |
smig rgyu dang |
mkha' snang dang |
mar me dang |
'bar ba dang |
zla ba dang |
nyi ma dang |
sgra gcan dang |
cha dang |
thig le mthong ba'i dbye bas ma brtags pa'i ye shes kyi phung po'o ||
bsam gtan mi bskyod pa nyid te rnam par shes pa'i phung po'o ||
rnam pa bcu ni yul dang yul can bcu po rnams gcig nyid du sna tshogs gzugs la bsam gtan no ||
srog rtsol yang na ral gri can ||
zhes pa 'du byed kyi phung po ste |
rnam pa bcu ni g.yon dang g.yas kyi dkyil 'khor bcu rnams gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa nyid kyi phyir ro ||
slar yang nam pa bcu ste 'dzin pa phyag na rin chen no ||
zhes pa tsor ba'i phung po ste |
srog gi 'dzin pa lte ba dang |
snying kha dang |
mgrin pa dang |
dpral ba dang |
gtsug tor gyi padma la 'gro ba dang 'ong ba'i dbye bas rnam pa bcu'o ||
g.yung mo la ni rjes su dran pa padma 'dzin par 'gyur te zhes pa 'du shes kyi phung po'o ||
g.yung mo dbu ma'i rtsa la rjes su dran pa de yang bcu ste 'dod pa'i gnas skabs bcu'i dbye ba las so ||
dpal ldan ting 'dzin 'khor lo can te rnam par snang mdzad do ||
ting nge 'dzin rnam pa bcu ni rlung bcu po nams 'gog pa'i phyir ro ||
de ltar bcom ldan 'das ni mi gnas pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa ste |
gang dag gi rlung rnams mi 'bab pas so zhes pa'i don to ||
da ni so sor sdud pa la sogs pa rnams kyi mtshan nyid bshad par bya ste |
so sor zhes pa la sogs pa'o ||
'dir so sor sdud pa zhes bya ba ni lus la yul dang yul can bcu po rnams kyi 'brel pas rnam par shes pa mi 'jug pa ste ||
stong pa nyid kyi yul rnams la mig la sogs pa gzhan Inga po rnams kyis rab tu 'jug pa'o ||
gzugs de nyid la shes rab ces pa lta ba dang | rtog pa zhes pa dngos po 'dzin pa dang |
dpyod pa ces pa de'i don nges par byed pa dang |
dga' ba zhes pa gzugs la chags pa dang |
g.yo med bde ba zhes pa gzugs dang lhan cig sems gcig tu byed pa'o ||
de ltar gzung ba dang |
'dzin pa'i dbye bas bsam gtan rnam pa bcu'o ||
'dir srog gi rtsol ba zhes bya ba yang lam ni gnyis po zhes pa g'yon pa dang g.yas pa'i lam nyams pa ste 'gog cing dbu ma'i lam du 'jug pa'o ||
de yang dkyil 'khor 'gog pa las rnam pa bcu'o ||
'dir thig ler zhes pa dpral bar srog rab tu 'jug pa gnyis ka'i bgrod pa bcom pa bzhes 'gro ba dang 'ong ba 'dzin pa srog dpral bar 'dzin pas sems rtse gcig pa zhes bya ba'o ||
gtum mo'i snang ba gang zhig srid pa gsum gyi nam mkha' las gyur pa de ni rjes su dran pa ste |
rnam pa bcu ni sngar gsungs zin to ||
shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid kyis ni zhes pa shes bya dang shes pa gcig tu 'dres pa nyid kyis te |
'gyur med bde ba'i dbang gis ye shes gzugs la ting nge 'dzin to ||
de yang srog la sogs pa nams med pa las rnam pa bcu'o ||
de ltar rnal 'byor yan lag drug gi sgrub byed so ||
'di rnams dman pa la sogs dbye ba nams kyis sna tshogs gtso bo dus kyi 'khor lo'i sgrub thabs rnam pa gsum du 'gyur |
phyag rgya gsum dang gnas skabs gsum ||
zhes pa ni |
rnam gsum bgrod pa'i dbang gis te ||
'dir byang chub kyi sems 'gyur ba'i bgrod pa ni dman pa'i gnas skabs so ||
g.yo ba'i bgrod pa ni 'bring po'o gnas skabs so |
mi g.yo ba'i bgrod pa ni mchog gi gnas skabs so ||
'di ltar las kyi phyag rgya ni 'gyur ba'i bde ba sbyin pa mo'o ||
ye shes kyi phyag rgya ni g.yo ba'i bde ba sbyin pa mo'o ||
phyag rgya chen mo ni mi g.yo ba'i bde ba sbyin pa mo'o ||
de ltar sbyor ba yan lag drug la phyag rgya gsum bsgom par bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa'i phyir rnal 'byor pas sangs rgyas nyid kyi ched du sbyor ba yan lag drug bsgom par bya'o zhes pa'o ||
de ni so sor sdud pa la sogs pa'i 'bras bu brjod par bya ste |
so sor zhes pa la sogs pa'o ||
'dir gang gi tshe rnal 'byor pa so sor sdud pa gzugs brtań par gyur pas nam par dag par 'gyur ba de'i tshe sngags rnams kun gyis byin gyis brlab par 'gyur te |
tshig gi mchog sbyin pa la sogs pa ster bar byed do ||
mngon par shes pa Inga ni thob par 'gyur te mi'i bdag bsam gťan sbyor bas dag pa'o ||
zhes pa ni 'dir gang gi tshe mig mi 'dzums par gyur pa de'i tshe lha'i mig tu 'gyur ro ||
de bzhin du lha'i rnam la sogs pa ste bsam gtan rnam par dag pa la 'gyur ro ||
srog gi 'tsol bas dag pa zhes pa ni 'dir gang gi tshe rnal 'byor pa nyi ma ri bong can gyi lam dang bral te |
rtag tu dbu mar 'bab par gyur pa de'i tshe srog gi rtsol bas dag par gyur pa ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis mchod de sngags par byed do ||
zhes pa'i don to ||
bdud dang nyon mongs la sogs nyams shing stob ma bcu la ni 'jug par 'gyur te zhes pa ni stong pa nyid kyi gzugs gzung ba la 'dzin pa'i sems 'jug par 'gyur te 'dzin pa'i stobs kyis so zhes pa ni srog gi 'gro ba dang 'ong ba zad pas gcig tu 'dres par 'gyur bas so ||
rjes su dran pa legs par dag pa zhes ni gzugs la 'khyud pa'i sems kyis rnam par rtog pa thams cad dang bral ba nyid de de las gang gi tshe dag par gyur pa de'i tshe dri med 'od kyi dkyil 'khor du 'gyur ro ||
kyang gi sgra las 'od zer Inga 'phro zhing ye shes gzugs las zhes pa stong pa'i gzugs las so ||
de nas ting nge 'dzin dag pa na zhes pa 'dir gzung ba dang 'dzin pa'i sems dag gcig nyid kyis gangs 'gyur ba med pa'i bde bar gyur pa'i bde ba de ni ting nge 'dzin du brjod par byas ste |
ting nge 'dzin de las dag pa dri ma med pa nyid du gyur pa ni nyin zhag nyung zad rnams kyis zhes pa phyogs gsum dang lo gsum rnams kyis ye shes lus ni grub par 'gyur zhes dbang bcu la sogs pa thob pa'i byang chub sems dpar 'gyur ro ||
de ltar so sor sdud pa la sogs pa'i nges pa'ol da ni stobs kyi rnal 'byor gsungs te |
'dir gang gi tshe so sor sdud pa la
sogs pa rnams kyi gzugs mthong bar gyur ba na srog ma bsdams pa nyid kyis 'gyur ba med pa'i skad cig mi skye ba de'i tshe nā da goms par 'chad par 'gyur ba'i stobs kyis srog dbu mar 'bab par byas nas shes rab kyi chu skyes su rdo rje nor bur byang chub kyi sems kyi thig le 'gog pa las mi g.yo bas mi 'gyur ba'i skad cig bsgrub par bya'o zhes pa stobs kyis rnal 'byor ro
nā da goms pa ni 'di nyid du bsgrub thabs kyi le'ur bcom Idan 'das kyis gsungs te
nus ma gang zhig lte ba'i dbus nas gzhan gyi gnas su bcu gnyis mtha' dang cha yin mthar 'gro ba //
de ni lte bar yang dag bkag nas glog gi me dang mtshungs pa'i dbyug pa'i gzugs can Ingas pa las //
'khor lo las ni 'khor lo gzhan du nges par dal zhing rol pa'i bgrod pas dbu ma'i rtsa la bskyod //
ji srid gtsug tor bug par stobs nyid kyis ni phyi rol pags pa la ni khab bzhin reg par byed
de yi dus su thur du sel ba mchog gi btsan thabs nyid kyis steng gi lam du bskul bar bya //
zung ni rlung dag 'gags pa na ni gtsug tor phug nas pha rol grong du 'gro bar 'gyur //
de ltar yid la rdo rje rab tu sad pas yul dang bcas pa mkha' la spyod pa nyid du 'gyur //
mngon shes Inga yi rang bzhin du 'gyur slar yang 'di ni rnal 'byor pa rnams dag gi sna tshogs yum
da ni stong pa nyid kyi gzugs sgrub pa'i ched du lta stangs brjod par bya ste
bsnyen pa zhes pa la sogs pa'o
'dir bsnyen pa zhes pa ni du ba la sogs pa'i mtshan ma sgom pa na ste
bsnyen pa de la dang po'i rnal 'byor ni sems kyi du ba la sogs pa'i mtshan ma 'dzin pa'o
de yang du ba la sogs pa dang lhan cig yid ches par byed pa rnam pa bcur 'gyur te des na rnam pa bcu'o
de ni 'khor lo can gyi zhes pa gtsug tor gyi khro bo'i lta bas zhes pa mig mi 'dzums par steng du lta bas mtshan mar 'gyur te
mtshan mo'i rnal 'byor pa gyis nam pa bzhi dang
nyin mo'i rnal 'byor gyis rnam pa drug ste de la gzugs kyi mthar thug pa so sor sdud pa dang bsam gtan gyis bsnyen pa'i yan lag tu 'gyur ro
bgegs mthar byed pa'i lta bas zhes pa la bgegs mthar byed ni bdud rtsi 'khyil ba'o
de'i lta bas ni bdud rtsi'i gnas su son pa ste dpral ba'i gnas su son pa'o
lta stangs des te bgegs mthar byed kyi bdud rtsi'i lam du son pas nye ba'i sgrub pa la yan lag drug go
kyang yig las srog rtsol dang 'dzin pa bya ste
'srog gi gzugs mthong bar gyur pa na nye ba'i sgrub pa'o
shes rab la spro zla ba thig le zhes pa la 'dir shes rab kyi chags pas spros pa yang 'di yin la
zla ba thig le yang yin pa ste
shes rab la spros pa'i byang chub kyi sems kyi thig le rdo rje nor bu son pa de'i yang gsang ba dang lte ba dang snying khar 'gyur med gsum du gyur pa gang yin pa de ni sgrub pa'i yan lag la zhes pa gsum sgrub pa'i yan lag la 'gyur ro
de ltar sgrub pa'i yan lag bya'o
bde ba ma nyams gcig pu zhi ba zhes pa bya ba la
'dir bde ba ma nyams pas byang chub kyi sems kyi skad cig gcig pu gang yin pa de ni zhi ba zhes brjod par bya ste
lhan skyes ye shes rnal 'byor 'dir ni sgrub pa chen po la zhes pa sems kyi 'gyur ba med pa'i bde ba dang lhan cig geig pa nyid do zhes pa bsgrub pa chen po'i yan lag ste bzhi pa'o
phyag rgya sgyu ma'i rjes su mthun pa yid dang nam mkha' la ni me long la ni gzugs bzhin te //
'jig rten gsum po snang bdu byed cing dri med glog dang mtshungs pa'i 'od zer du ma 'phro bar byed
phyi dang lus la dbyer med yul dang rnam par bral ba tsam ste mka' la gnas pa ni //
sems kyi rang bzhin 'gro ba du ma'i ngo bo'i sems la 'khyud par byed do de ni gcig pu'o
sems kyi snang ba tsam ste rang gi yid las skyes pa me long gi ni gzugs brnyan lta bu nyid //
gang zhig mtha' dag rgyal ba'i sras dang sangs rgyas rnams kyis bsten pa rnal 'byor dbang pos bsten bya ste //
ye shes me lce rab tu rgyas pa de ni bdud rtsogs yul dang bcas pa mtha' dag sreg byed cing //
lus la 'dod chags la sogs kyang ste rnal 'byor pa la lo yi sbyor bas bde mnyam ster bar byed
shes rab ye shes kyang ste sems dang de yi snang ba nyid ni rnam pa bcu por 'gyur
dbang bskur dri med ri bong can mtshungs gzugs brnyan Ita bur 'di la zhugs pa gang yin pa //
de las mya ngan 'das pa'i bde ba 'pho med lhan cig skyes pa 'gyur med nyid ni bzhi pa ste //
sangs rgyas zhal 'di gang gi snying dang kha la gnas pa de ni dpal Idan bla ma'o
S'dir shes rab ye shes ni grangs bzhin du 'dzin par byed pa'i sems dang sems kyi gzung bar bya gang yin pa du ba la sogs pa nams bcu po ste me long gi snang ba pra phab pa lta bu de nyid ni ye shes te gzung bar bya ba'i sems so zhes pa'i don to
de ltar me long rang gi mig dang gzugs brnyan ni mig dang gzung bar bya ba yin pa de bzhin du shes rab dang ye shes kyang ste
dbang bskur 'di la zhugs pa gang yin pa zhes pa ni 'dir 'dzin par byed pa'i sems kyi gzung bar bya ba'i sems de la rab tu zhugs pa ni phyi rol gyi yul rnams la rab tu mi 'jug pa'o
yan lag drug la so sor sdud dang bsam gtan dang srog rtsol dang 'dzin pa ni zhugs bde ba 'pho med lhan cig skyes pa mi 'gyur nyid ni bzhi pa ste
bde ba byis pa dang dar la bab pa dang g'yo ba rnams kyi mchog 'jig rten pa'i dpe las 'das pa 'jig rten gsum gyi spyod pa las grol ba zhes pa'i don to

lag beangs dang
gnyis gnyis bral ba las kyi phyag rgya dang
ye shes kyi phyag rgya'i rgyud dang bral ba stong pa nyid rnam pa thams cad pa so sor snang pa'o zhes pa'o
sangs rgyas zhal 'di ste ye shes kyi zhal slob dpon gang gi snying la gnas pa zhes pa myangs shing rang gis nyams su myong ba dang
slob ma rnams la rtogs su gzhug pa'i ched du dus thams cad du kha la gnas par gyur pa de ni dpal ldan bla ma ste rdo rje 'dzin pa'o zhes pa'i don to
gzhan dbang po gnyis kyi bde ba rtogs par byed pa po rnams ni ma yin no zhes pa'o
naṃ mkhar kun nas chags pa'i sems kyis mig ni mi 'dzums rdo rje'i lam du zhugs pa yis //
stong pa las ni du ba smig rgyu dri ma med pa'i mkha' snang rab gsal nyid dang mar me dang //
'bar ba dang ni zla ba nyi ma rdo rje rnams dang mchog gi cha dang thig le mthong bar 'gyur
de yi dbus su ye shes gzugs ni yul dang rnam par bral ba longs spyod rdzogs sku du ma'o
da ni rang gi sems 'di'i rang gi snang ba la rab tu gzhug pa gsungs te
naṃ mkhar zhes pa la sogs pa'o
'dir sngags kyi theg pa dang pha rol tu phyin pa'i theg pa la rnal 'byor goms pa rnam pa gnyis te
naṃ mkha' la dang
bla gab med pa la'o
gang zhig naṃ mkha' la rnal 'byor goms par byed pa des mtshan mo bug pa med pa'i khang par mun pa la
naṃ mkha' kun nas chags pa'i sems dang mig mi 'dzums shing rdo rje'i lam du zhugs pa zhes pa 'dir dbu mar srog rab tu zhugs pas stong pa kho na las du ba la sogs pa'i mtshan ma mthong ste
kun nas zhen pa'i sems dang mig mi 'dzums shing rdo rje'i lam du zhugs pa yis //
stong pa la ni du ba smig rgyu rab gsal dri ma med pa'i mkha'
snang nyid dang mar me dang | zhes pa ni mtshan mo'i rnal 'byor gyis mthong ngo
de nas nam mkha' sprin med pa la Ita ste
nam mkha' de la nyin mo'i rnal 'byor gyis | nam mkha' las byung rang 'byung ba | shes rab ye shes me bo che | zhes pa 'bar ba mthong ste | sprin med pa'i nam mkha' la'o
de bzhin du 'od chen nam par snang bar byed | ye shes snang ba lam me ba| | zhes pa zla ba'i snang ba'o
'gro ba'i mar me ni nyi ma'o | | ye shes sgron ni rdo rje ste | sgra gcan gzi brjid chen po'o
'od gsal ni glog ste mchog gi cha shes pa'o l //
sngags mchog dbang po rig sngags rgyal | ni thig le'o
de ltar mtshan ma mam pa bcu ste | 'dus pa la sogs par mtshan mo'i rnal 'byor gyis dang | mtshan yang dag par brjod par nyin mo'i rnal 'byor gyis bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs so
de nas | gsang sngags rgyal po don chen byed | ces pa nam pa thams cad pa bum pa dang snam bu la sogs pa'i gzugs brnyan mthong ngo zhes pa'o
de'i dbus su ste thig le'i dbus su sangs rgyas gzugs ni yul dang rnam par bral ba ste de'i rdzas med pa'i phyir dang | rtog pa med pa'i phyir longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku du ma'o
de nas gzugs brnyan gyis rnal 'byor gyis gzhom du med pa'i sgra gang yin pa de kho na thos te de ltar gzugs snang ba las sprul pa'i sku dang sgra snang ba las longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku'o zhes bya ba'i don to
| nam mkha' chu 'dzin bral bar gyur pa rnal 'byor pa yis rengs pa'i mig gis blta bya ste
|| re khā nag po dri med 'od zer 'phro ba dus kyi rtsa la mthong gyur par du'o
|| de la kun mkhyen gzugs ni chu la nyi ma lta bur rnyog pa med pa sna tshogs mdog
|| rnam pa kun ldan rang sems yul dang nam par bral ba'i sems ni gzhan pa min pa nyid
X'dir nyin mo'i rnal 'byor ni nam mkha' chu 'dzin bral ba rnal 'byor pa'i rengs pa'i mig gis blta bar bya ba ste | snga dro dang phyi dro nyi ma la rgyab bstan nas so
|| rnam pa gzhan du na nyi ma'i 'od zer rnams kyis mig rab rib tu 'gyur te ngo de yis te gnod pa las so
|| nyin mo so sor blta bar bya ste ji srid thig le'i dbus su ri mo nag po skra'i tshad tsam pa dri med 'od zer 'phro ba dus kyi rtsa la ste a wa dhū tī la mthong bar gyur kyi bar du'o
|| nang du 'dus pa kun mkhyen gzugs ni khams gsum ma lus chu nang la nyi ma bzhin du rnyog pa med pa kha dog sna tshogs te rnam pa kun ldan rang sems yul dang bral ba rnams so
|| sems ni gzhan pa min pa nyid
gzhan gyi sems ni ma yin te gzhan gyi sems shes pa med pa'i phyir ro
|| 'dir dang por rang gi sems kyi snang ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i sha'i spyan gyis mthong zhing sha'i spyan dang lha'i spyan dang sangs rgyas kyi spyan dang shes rab kyi' dang ye shes kyi spyan ni sgoms pa'i stobs kyis 'byung bar 'gyur ro
|| de'i phyir thams cad mkhyen pas ma gzigs pa cung zad kyang med do |
x sa yi rnam par sra ba min zhing zhu ba chu lus khu ba min te gsher ba nyid min phyir
|| me yi rnam pa me min g.yo ba rlung gi lus ni gang zhig g.yo ba min pas rlung ma yin
|| stong pa'i rnam pa yin yang lta bya dkar dang ljang dang kha dog sna tshogs chen po kha dog min
|| rnam pa thams cad yin yang blta bya min te rang snying dri ma nyon mongs bdud kyi mthu las so
sa ni chu la rab tu 'jug cing chu yang 'bar byed la ste gtsang mar byed pa rlung la'o
|| rlung ni stong pa la ste stong pa nges par mtshan ma rnam pa bcu ru 'gyur zhing mtshan ma ni
|| rnam pa thams cad pa ru 'gyur te mi 'gyur mchog gi bde ba gzhom du med pa'i ye shes sku |
ye shes las ni rdzu 'phrul dngos grub tu 'gyur mi bdag skyes bu rnams kyi skye ba 'di nyid la'o |
dbu mar srog ni rab gzhug nyi ma dang bcas ri bong can gyi bgrod pa g.yas dang g.yon la bcing |
|| phyag rgya rab tu 'grogs la sems dang mchog gi bde ba la gnas rdo rje yang dag bslang ba yang
|| padmar rdo rje'i sgra 'am rang gi lag pa chu las skyes pas gsor ba bde ba'i rgyu ru ste ſ sa bon gtong ba min pa'i bde ba de ni 'chi ba'i 'jigs 'phrog 'di ni dpal ldan bla ma'i zhal
phyag rgya chen po'i man ngag 'di ni mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas kyis kyang rab tu gsungs pa 'di lta ste | 'di nyid kyi ni sgrub byed bya l snang brnyan bsams pa ma yin pa | du sogs mtshan ma de rnams kyis | shes rab gzugs brnyan nam mkha' mnyam |
| yod dang med las rnam par 'das |
I rang gi sems kyi yid ches don |
I rdul phran las skyes tshogs pa rnams |
I kun nas yongs su dor ba yis |
I du ba smig rgyu mkha' snang dang |
| sgron ma 'bar ba zla nyi dang |
I mun can cha dang thig le che |
| sna tshogs gzugs brnyan 'od gsal ba |
| btsums shing ma btsums mig dag gis |
I stong par gang zhig rjes brtags min.l
I rmi lam bzhin du gzugs mthong 'gyur |
I gzugs de rtag tu bsgom par bya |
I dngos med gzugs la sgom pa de l
| rnal 'byor pa yis sgom ma yin |
I dngos po dngos med sems kyis min |
I gzugs la ma brtags mthong phyir ro |
| ji ltar pra phab gzhon nu mas |
I me long la mthong dngos skyes min |
I de bzhin 'das dang ma 'ongs chos |
| mkha' la de nyid rnal 'byor pas |
I de'i las dngos po dngos med 'gyur |
I dngos po stong pa'i don mthong phyir |
| dngos las dngos med don yod pa l
| sgyu ma rmi lam mig 'phrul bzhin |
I med pa'i chos can la ni chos |
I skye ba 'di ni rab tu mthong |
| yid bzhin nor ltar mtha' yas pa'il

| rang gi gdong pa cis mi mthong | I gal te med pa'i gzugs mthong na | I ri bong rwa ni cis mi mthong |
| gzhan gyi mig gi mi mthong zhing | I rang gi mig gis mthong min pa | I skye ba med pa mthong ba ni | I gzhon nu ma'i bu ji bzhin
adslar yang gang gis sangs rgyas nyid | 'grub pa'i thabs de brjod par bya ste || rnal 'byor pa nyid ces pa la sogs pa'o ||
'dir rnal 'byor pa rnam pa gnyis te | 'jig rten pa dang 'jig rten pa las 'das pa'o ||
de las 'jig rten las 'das pa'i rnal 'byor pa ni sangs rgyas nyid do ||
di'i khyad par ni bsod nams mchog ste bsod nams kyi rtshogs yongs su rdzogs pa nyid kyi phyir ro ||
thabs 'dis rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi sangs rgyas nyid 'grub par 'gyur ro zhes pa lung gi brjod pa'o ||
thabs 'di ni rnal 'byor yan lag drug ste gcig gi bar bcad pa'i yan lag re dor nas dbus ma rim pa ma yin pas gsungs so ||
ma brjod pa yan lag snga ma de nyid ni snga ma dang phyi ma brjod pa'i yan lag gi shugs nyid las rtogs par bya ste | dper na 'di nyin mor byed pa'o zhes brjod pa na gzhan mtshan mor byed pa zhes ma brjod kyang shugs nyid las nye bar thob pa bzhin no ||
de ltar phan tshun ltos pa'i chos ni ma brjod pa'i chos las thob bo ||
de'i phyir rgyud 'dir re res par bcang pa'i yan lag phyi ma gsum bstan te | dbus su gsungs pa'i yan lag snga mar bya ba ni 'dir bla ma'i gdams ngag las so zhes rig par bya ste | thams cad du de yis rtogs par bya'o ||
yang na byang chub sems dpas mdzad pa'i rgya cher 'grel gyis te | rnam pa gzhan du mkhas pa ni nyid kyi mngon pa'i nga rgyal du gyur pa rnams kyis byas pa'i 'grel pas ni ma yin no zhes pa'o ||
de'i phyir 'dir yan lag gsum bsam gtan dang zhes pa la sogs pa des rtogs par bya'o ||
rgyud gzhan du gsungs pa'i yan lag drug po rnams kyis 'dir yang mdor byas pa'i don yan lag gsum gyis rtogs par bya'o ||
'dir 'dus pa phyi mar bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa l I so sor sdud dang bsam gtan dang | I de bzhin srog rtsol 'dzin pa dang | | rjes su dran dang ting 'dzin te | I rnal 'byor yan lag drug tu 'dod | I ces so ||
de'i phyir bsam gtan gyi sngon du so sor sdud pa rig par bya'o l |
sngags bzlas pa'i sngon du srog rtsol rig par bya'o ||
'dir sngags bzlas pa'i sgras ma ning gi bzlas pa 'am rdo rje'i bzlas pa ste srog 'dzin pa brjod bya'o ||
bde ba'i sngon du rjes su dran pa rig par bya ste | 'dir bde ba'i sgras ting nge 'dzin brjod par bya'o ||
de ltar yan lag drug po 'di rnams kyis rnal 'byor pa sangs rgyas nyid du 'grub par 'gyur ro ||
thun mong gis phyi rol gyi rnal 'byor pa nyid 'jig rten pa 'grub po ||
las dang po pa byis pa'i rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis ni sngags bzlas pas te so sor sdud pa'i ming can gyis dang bsam gtan gyis te dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo la sogs pa sgom pa'i sems dang bde ba yis te | las kyi phyag rgya dang ye shes kyi phyag rgya 'gyur ba dang g.yo ba'i bde bas 'jig rten pa'i bde bas 'og min gyi srid pa'i mthar thug pa zhes pa'o ||
de ltar rim pa gnyis kyis 'jig rten pa dang | 'jig rten las 'das pa'i rnal 'byor pa nyid 'grub po ||
'dir 'jig rten las 'das pa'i rnal 'byor pa nyid yan lag drug gi rnal 'byor gyis 'grub par 'gyur ji ltar de ltar brjod par bya'o l
Bẞ'dir so sor sdud pa zhes bya ba ni phyi rol gyi gzugs la sogs pa'i yul rnams la mig la sogs pa'i dbang po rnams kyis mig gi rnam par shes pa la sogs pa nams mi 'jug par nang gi yul rnams la lha'i mig la sogs pa'i dbang po rnams kyis lha'i mig gi rnam par shes pa la sogs pa rnams 'jug pa ste | nang du stong pa nyid la dmigs pas ni dngos po thams cad stong pa la mthong ba ma brtags pa pra phab pas me long la gzhon nu mas mthong ba lta bur 'gyur ro zhes pa so sor sdud pa'i yan lag tu brjod de | khams gsum pa'i sangs rgyas kyi gzugs brnyan mthong ba'i phyir ro ||
de nas bsam gtan zhes bya ba ni stong pa'i chos thams cad mthong bar 'gyur ba la shes rab ces bya ba 'di nams la sems 'jug pa dang | rtog pa zhes bya ba sems kyi dngos po 'dzin pa dang dpyod pa zhes bya ba dngos po rtogs pa dang | dga' ba zhes bya ba dngos po thams cad la sems gnas pa dang | g.yo med bde ba zhes bya ba dngos po thams cad la sems gnas pa dang | g.yo med bde ba zhes bya ba dngos po thams cad las bde ba kun nas srog rtsol zhes bya ba la la nā dang | ra sa nā g.yon dang g.yas pa'i lam 'gags pas dbu ma a wa dhū tī'i lam du srog gi rlung rtag tu 'jug pa ste | dgang ba dang bum pa can dang | dbyung pa'i sbyor bas a wa dhū rīr ru om yig gis 'jug pa dang | huṃ yig gis 'gog pa dang ah yig gis 'byung ba zla ba dang | sgra gcan dang | nyi ma'i rang bzhin gyis rnal 'byor pas byed do zhes pa'o ||
de nas 'dzin pa zhes bya ba srog dbang chen dang | chu dang me dang rlung gi dkyil 'khor la lte ba dang | snying ga dang | mgrin pa dang | dpral bar zhugs pa phyi rol du 'gro ba med par thig le la srog 'jug pa zhes bya ba 'dzin pa'i yan lag tu brjod par bya'o ||

dang zhing ma skyes ma 'gags mchog gi bde byed rnyog med ye shes kyis ni rtogs bya ste ||
gzhan gyis gzung bya khams gsum du ni mtha' dag 'gro ba'i dbang po'i sgo yis rtogs bya min |
I sangs rgyas nams kyi 'ang rtogs bya min pa dpag med yon tan dang ldan sangs rgyas spru kha pa sgyu ma ni ||

sna tshogs dngos rnams kyis phye rgyal ba dang bcas lha mi rnams kyi rang rang sems la rab zhugs pa ||
'di ni ma skyes chos te chu la nam mkha' bzhin du 'di la skye bar 'khrul pa ster ba'o |
| khro bo sangs rgyas lha mo dang bcas nges par ro yi rdo rje rnams dang sa yi snying po sogs ||

bod skad du |
rnal 'byor yan lag drug gi brjed byang yon tan gyis 'gengs pa zhes bya ba |
dpal dus kyi 'khor lo la phyag 'tshal lo |
I dpal dus 'khor lo dbang phyug dang ni yon tan gyis |

yon tan rnams kyis 'gengs par byed pa'i brjed byang bya |

dpal kha sarpa ni'i drung du lo bcu gnyis bzhugs par gyur to ||

bu bkri ma'i grong du song shig |

yan lag drug mdzad par bzhed pas ||

rgya cher 'grel des gsal byas pa'i
sangs rgyas rin chen snying po ste
yang dag pa'i gdams ngag gis lam snying po bsod nams nyid las te
bla ma dam pa'i skabs las thob nas nges pa'i don gyi bdud rtsi bsten pas te
don dam pa dga' bzhi pa'i bdud rtsi thob pas srid pa'i dug ste
skye zhing skye ba'i dug chen po gcod par byed cing mchog tu mi 'gyur ba 'dren par byed do
de nyid kyi phyir
dga' bar byed de rtag tu lhun gyis grub pa'i bde ba rgyun mi 'chad pa'i phyir ro
'dis ni rgya cher 'grel mdzad pa po'i yang ches che ba nyid gsal bar byed do
slar yang rgyud kyi lhag pa'i rgyal po'i bdag nyid che ba nyid ston par byed de I shes bya shes so
shes bya ba'i tshogs rnams kyi ste l phyi dang nang gi rig pa'i gnas kyi tshogs mtha' dag gi gzhi rten te 'byung gnas su'o
gsungs pa yang dang po'i sangs rgyas su ma gsungs pa gang de med do shes so
rgyud kyi rgyal po gang du bcom ldan 'das kyi ste I sangs rgyas kyi kun mkhyen gyi mtshan thams cad mkhyen pa zhes pa'i mngon brjod rjes mthun pa ste
don gyi rjes su mthun par gyur pa dang de kho na nyid rin po che'i 'byung gnas der dbang phyug la sogs pas bshad pa ni rtsa ba dang 'dra ba ste
g.yo bzhin pa nyid kyis na snying po med pa nyid kyi phyir rtswar gyur pa'o
rgyud gang du shes rab pha rol phyin pa 'ang ste chos thams cad stong pa nyid rtogs par byed pa yang ni gang du thal mtha' dag kyang de yin la
'du shes mang por gyur pa gang yin pa'i rnam par rtog pa'i ljon pa nams te
de rnams rab tu 'jig byed mchog tu mi 'gyur ba ro mnyam par gyur pa yin pa de ni de skad do
nam mkha' las byung ni sna tshogs gzugs las byung ba'i'o
dag pa'i bdag nyid de ni rnam rnam par rtog pa mtha' dag gis mtshan ma med pa'i sku can gyi phyir ro
da ni du ba la sogs pa'i mtshan ma byung ba'i rgyu rnal 'byor yan lag drug gi rten dpal ldan bla mas yang dag par rab tu byin pa'i man ngag gsungs pa dri med ces so
dri ma med pa'i nam mkha' [P1, 346b] gang la ste
sprin la sogs pa'i sgrib pa dang bral ba'o
sprul pa la ste nang lad la dmigs pa'i bya ba can la'oll mig phyed la ste bkab cing bkab pa'i mig la'o
snying rje chen po rgyas pas brlan cing snum par byed pa'i bsam pa las te
sems las rten rnam par rtog pa'i sems za zhing rang bzhin med par byed de zhes pa de las so

pho nya kun gyi lhan cig skyes pa 'thob ste rtsa stong phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis 'gags pa'i rang bzhin gyi mkha' 'gro ma rnams kyi stong pa ni lhan cig skyes pa zhes brjod cing srid pa gsum spangs pa'i gzhom du med pa yin gyi | 'jig rten las grags pa'i bzhi pa'i bdag nyid kyi lhan cig skyes pa ni ma yin no ||

de yang 'di yin la dngos grub kyang ngo zhes pa'o ||

|| de'i ste dngos po'i don nges par byed pa ni stong pa nyid kyi ngo bo nyid du nges par 'dzin pa'o
|| gzugs la chags pa ni yul bzlog pas yid tsam du nang du phyogs pas lha'i bdag nyid la dmigs bzhin pas bla na med pa'i shes rab dang thabs ro gcig par rnam par dpyod pa las de kho na nyid la 'jug cing mngon du phyogs pas gang yid bde ba nyid de de ni dga' ba ste rangs pa'o zhes pa'i don to
|| lus shin tu spyangs pa la sogs pa'i bde ba ni g.yo med bde ba'o
|| dbu ma'i lam du rab tu 'jug pa zhes pa ni nam mkha' la sogs pa dkyil 'khor bcu 'jig pas brkyang ma dang ro ma dag 'gags pa'i dbu ma'i lam kho nar rab tu 'jug pa dang gnas pa dang 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid can srog gi rlung phra mo 'jug pa ni srog rtsol lo zhes pa'i bar ro
|| gnyis ka'i bgrod pa bcom pa zhes pa ni lte ba dang snying ga dang | mgrin pa dang | dpral ba dang | gtsug tor rnams su 'gro ba dang 'ong ba dang bral bas 'gro bar byed pa dang bral ba'o zhes byas nas
| rim pas rlung rnams med pa la thig le rnams la ste byang chub kyi sems kyi thig le la sems kyi bzhon pa srog gi rlung stong pa nyid dang ro gcig pa nyid ni 'dzin pa'o zhes gsungs par 'gyur ro
|| rnam pa bcu ni sngar gsungs zin to
zhes pa ni 'dzin pa la rab tu zhugs pa'i sems la rang gi ngang gis du ba la sogs pa'i mtshan ma nam pa bcu so sor snang ba sngon ma can rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi lha mthong ba gzugs brnyan gyi rnam pa can rtog pa med pa dang | de las 'od zer du ma spro ba dang | de nas khams gsum du lha'i 'khor lo spro ba sngon ma can bsam pa la sogs pa 'dod pa'i gnas skabs bcu ni rjes su dran pa'o zhes smras pa'o
|| srog la sogs pa rnams med pa las zhes pa gtum mo 'bar bas yang dag par gsal bar byas pa nyid kyis rdo rje nor bu'i mgo bo'i rtse mo rab tu gnas pa'i byang chub sems ma nyams ste
de nas rims kyis 'khor lo drug rnams su dbugs brgya phrag drug lhag pa'i stong phrag nyi shu rtsa gcig 'gags pa nyid kyis rnam par rtog pa med pa'i rnal 'byor mi 'gyur ba'i bde ba chen po'i rang bzhin zung 'jug gi bdag nyid kyi ye shes kyi rang bzhin gyi sku rdo rje 'chang chen po'i ngo bo ni ting nge 'dzin zhes pa'o
|| so sor sdud pa la sogs pa'i 'bras bu ni yan lag drug gi rjes su bsngags pa'i mtshan nyid do
|| byang chub sems dpa' 'gyur ro zhes pa ni skye ba dang byang chub pa dang chos kyi 'khor lo la sogs pa rab tu mi 'jug pa'i phyir te
de rab tu 'jug pas ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas su tha snyad bya ba bsten par 'gyur ba kho na'o zhes dgongs pa'o
|| dbang bcu la sogs pa zhes pa tshe dang | las dang mos pa dang | sems dang | yo byad dang | smon lam dang | rdzu 'phrul dang | skye ba dang | ye shes dang | chos la dbang ba la sogs pa'o
|| gzung ba dang 'dzin pa'i sems ni byed pa po'o
|| gzhan du yang rjes su bsngags pa gsungs pa I so sor sdud pas snyoms 'jug pa
| sngags rnams kun gyis byin gyis rlob
| | bsam gtan ye shes snyoms 'jug pas
| | mngon shes Inga ni thob par 'gyur

rtag tu 'dzin pa'i stobs kyis ni
| I rdo rje sems dpa' mnyam par 'jug

| | sgrib pa med par Idan par 'gyur

|| dman pa nyid kyis g.yon pa dang g.yas par 'bab par bar chad pa'i phyir srog ma bsdams pa nyid kyis so
|| nā da goms pa las ye shes lhan cig skyes pa'i dga' ba goms pa las so
|| stobs kyis te hūm yig gi nā das shes rab kyi chu skyes dang rdo rje nor bur byang chub kyi sems kyi thig le 'gog pa las te
ji skad bshad pa'i 'dod dam ma mchog rjes su chags pa can gyi kakko la ru cho ga bzhin du bo la bcug nas g.yo ba med pa nyid kyis da' gso? ste
de'i ze 'bru'i sgo ru rdo rje nor bu bcir nas shin tu dga' ba'i ro yis skad cig g.yo ba med par bya'o
|| de nas de dag gis 'gyur ba'i skabs byung na hūm yig brjod pa mngon du 'gro bar bla ma'i zhabs kyi bka' drin gyis byas pa la mngon du phyogs shing bzhi pa lhan cig skyes pa'i dga' ba nyams su myong bar byang chub kyi sems kyi thig le 'gog pa ste I de nas 'gyur ba med pa'i skad cig mthong ba'o zhes pa'i don to
|| dpal dus kyi 'khor lo 'di nyid du 'brel pa can du gang zhes pa la sogs pa ste
nus ma ni srog gi nus pa'o
|| gzhan gyis gnas su 'gro ste dmigs pa med pa'i gnas su'o
|| bcu gnyis mtha' zhes pa sna bug g.yon pa nam mkha' la sogs pa sa'i dkyil 'khor gyi mthar thug par sor bcu drug la sogs par sor bcu gnyis kyi mthar thug par ro
|| cha'i mtha' zhes pa sna bug g.yas par sa'i dkyil 'khor la sogs pa nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor gyi mthar thugs pa sor bcu gnyis la sogs pa sor bcu drug gi par du'o
|| hum yig brjod pas srog dang thur sel gyi rlung dag sbyor ba las de lte bar bkag nas srog gi nus pa de nyid glog gi me dang mtshungs shing glog gi me dang 'dra ba'o
|| bskyod pa ni bsgul ba'o
|| khab bzhin ni khab dang 'dra ba'o
|| thur du sel ba zhes pa 'gro'o ste rab tu 'jug go
|| pha rol gyi grong du lte lus gzhan du'o
|| ci ste 'di tsam gyis kyang gzhan gyi grong du 'jug pa ma yin cir 'gyur zhe na
de'i tshe 'di ltar bya'o zhes gsungs pa
'di ltar te rim pa 'di yis rdo rje rab tu sad pa las te mchog tu bde ba'i ye shes rab tu sad pa las so
|| yid la zhes pa gzung ba dang 'dzin pa'i sems gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa'i sems la'o
|| yul dang bcas pa ni dbang po dang yul gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa nus pa de'o
|| mkha' la spyod ni 'og min gyi sa'i mthar thug pa la dbang byed pa'i bdag po nyid du 'gro'o
|| 'ba' zhig tu ma zad do zhes gsungs pa
mngon shes zhes pa la sogs pa ste rdzu 'phrul dang lha'i
mig dang lha'i rna ba dang gzhan gyi sems shes pa dang sngon gyi gnas rjes su dran pa'i rang bzhin ni mngon shes lnga'i rang bzhin no ||
sna tshogs yum du yang 'gyur te phyag rgya chen mor shes rab gyi chags pas rnam par rtog pa med pa'i gtum mo 'bar bas 'gyur med gsum gyi rnal 'byor zhes pa gsang bar son pa sems kyi thig le 'gags pa dang | lte bar son pa ngag gi thig le 'gags pa zhes pa'o ||
bde ba ma nyams pas rdo rje'i sgor thig le'i tshogs ma 'phos pas ye shes kyi thig le 'gags pa ye shes kyi rnal 'byor ro ||
zung gi rlung gi dgag pa 'di ni zhes pa'i mthar thug pas sa la spyod pa'i dngos grub gsal bar mdzad cing mkha' la spyod pa nyid du 'gyur zhes pa'i mthar thug pas mtha' la spyod pa'i dngos grub rtogs par mdzad la | sna tshogs yum zhes pa'i bar gyis phyag rgya chen po'i dngos grub gsal bar mdzad do zhes pa'ol |
phyag rgya zhes pa ni phyag rgya chen mo'o ||
sgyu ma'i rjes su mthun pa ni shin tu dang ba'i me long gi gzugs brnyan lta bu'o ||
yid dang nam mkha' la yang ni sems med pa'i sems pa'o ||
de nyid kyi phyir gsungs pa | me long la ni gzugs bzhin zhes pa ste | gzugs bzhin ni gzugs dang 'dra bar 'phro ba'o ||
phyi dang lus la dbyer med pa yul dang dbang po dag ga gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa'o ||
chos can dang tha dad par chos snang ba'i ngo bo nyid kyi phyir yul dang nam par bral ba'o ||
du ma'i ngo bo 'gro ba'i sems la 'khyud par byed de de gcig pu spang ngo ||
de nyid kyi phyir sems kyis rang bzhin te sems med pa'i sems kyi rang bzhin no ||
sems kyi ste gzung ba dang 'dzin pa dag gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa'i snang ba tsam rang gi lus la snang par ni gyi ngo bo mchog gi phyag rgya'o ||
rang gi yid la ste rnam pa thams cad dang rnam pa med pa'i ngo bo la skyes pa ste byung ba'o ||
de ni ye shes me lce 'bar ba ste ye shes kyi gtum mo 'bar ba 'phel ba'o ||
yul dang bcas pa ste phung po dang khams dang skye mched kyi 'khor lo dang bcas pa'i bdud tshogs te rnam par rtog pa'i sems kyi dra ba'o ||
gsungs pa yang bdud ni rang gi sems te bdud gzhan yod pa ma yin zhes so ||
ster zhing rtsol te zhi ba'i bde ba ni lhan cig skyes pa'i dga' ba'i bde ba'o ||
lo'i sbyor ba las te lo gsum dang phyogs gsum gyi dus kyi 'khor lo'i so zhes pa'i don to ||
shes rab ni ye shes zhes pa grangs ji lta ba bzhin te | shes rab ni chos thams cad stong pa nyid du rtogs pa ste de ni sems so ||
de'i du ba la sogs pa'i kun nas mthong ba'i snang ba'i gzung bya'i sems te de kho na'i ye shes so ||
'dis ni ji ltar me long la gzugs brnyan rang gi mig gis te bdag nyid kyi mig gis gzung bar bya ba de ltar stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin kyi sems kyis stong pa nyid kyis rang bzhin rnam pa thams cad pa'i sems rtag tu mthong ba'o zhes pa'i don to ||
gzung ba'i sems du ba la sogs pa'i mtshan nyid can 'di la 'dzin pa'i sems stong pa nyid zhugs shing gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa'o ||
rjes su dran pa gsungs pa' dri med ces pa'o ||
byis pa dang dar la bab pa dang g.yo ba rnams kyi zhes pa thabs kyi g.yo ba ste phyag rgya las byung ba zhes pa'o ||
rdo rje 'dzin pa ni rdo rje 'dzin pa'i sprul pa'i sku'o zhes pa'i don to ||
de ni rang gi sems 'di'i snang ba la ste | me long gi gzugs brnyan dang mtshungs pa'i sems kyi snang ba la gzhug pa brjod par bya ste | nam mkha' zhes pa la sogs pa'o ||
mtshan mo zhes pa mun pa nye bar mtshon pa'o ||
nam mkhar kun nas zhen pa'i sems ni gang rnams kyi sems nam mkha' la kun nas chags pa ste | de dang ro mnyam par gyurt pa yin pa de rnams kyis so | |
rdo rje'i lam du zhugs pas ni srog a va dhū tīr son pa nyid kyi phyir ro ||
nam mkhar sprin med pa ni mtshan ma ni gzhi can gyi ngo bo'i phyir ro ||
de ltar gzugs snang ba las zhes pa me long gi gzugs brnyan Ita bu nyog pa med cing thogs pa med pa'i gzugs so sor snang ba las so ||
sgra snang ba las zhes pas mgrin pa dang rkang la sogs pa'i rtsol ba las mi skyes pa'i bde ba chen po rol pa gzhom du med pa'i sgra tsam nyams su myong ba las so ||
'di tsam gyis ni tshigs su bcad pa 'dis bcom Idan 'das dpal rdo rje 'dzin pa'i sku gnyis gsal bar byas par 'gyur ro ||
nam mkha' zhes pa'ilo la rengs pa'i mig gis ni mig g.yo ba med pas so ||
gzhan gyi sems shes pa med pa'i phyir de rang gi sems las tha dad pa'i gzhan gyi sems shes par bya ba med pa'i phyir ro ||
gsungs pa | yang I ma skyes pa dang ma 'gags pa'i | I shes bya gang zhig 'dir mthong ba | I de ni rang sems gzhan gyi min | | phyi'i shes bya rnam 'byed phyir | I de phyir bdag gis bdag nyid la | I 'gar yang rgyas gdab nus ma yin | | ral gri chen po bdag nyid la | | bdag nyid so yis gcod dam ci | | me gsham bu mo dpa' grogs pas | I rmi lam du ni bde 'gyur ltar | I nam mkha' las byung gzugs brnyan ni | | bsten pas bdag la de bzhin no | | zhes so ||
tshigs su bcad pa 'dis chos kyi sku'i sgrub par byed pa gsungs par 'gyur ro ||
sa'i rnam pa zhes pa la sogs pa gzugs kyi mtshan nyid de | 'dir stong pa nyid kyi so sor snang ba rdzas ma yin pa sa la sogs pa la mthong ste sra ba me yin no ||
zhu ba'i rnam pa can smig rgyu'i chu lta bu ni chu ma yin te gsher ba min nyid phyir ro ||
me'i rnam pa ni de bzhin du me min te gang gi phyir ba sreg par byed pa med pas so ||
g.yo ba rlung gis lus ni rdzas med pa nyid kyi phyir gang g.yo ba med pa de ni rlung ma yin no ||
rdzas med pa nyid kyi phyir stong pa'i rnam pa ste I de yin yang blta bar bya'o ||
sgyu ma'i grong khyer bzhin du dkar po la sogs pa'i kha dog dang ldan yang rdzas med pa'i phyir kha dog ma yin no ||
rnam pa thams cad pa yin yang dus thams cad du byis pa rnams kyi lta bya min no ||
